# wf-c s07-retry

Started: 2026-10-11T08:28:46.832458+00:00

## User prompt

```text
Goal: replace SQLAlchemy with Tortoise ORM across the Mealie backend.

Fixed decisions:
- Use Tortoise ORM, which is async. When the rewrite is finished, nothing under mealie/
  should import SQLAlchemy.
- Keep support for both SQLite and PostgreSQL.
- Replace Alembic with Aerich. Upgrading existing databases from the old Alembic history
  is out of scope.
- Keep the HTTP API and the Pydantic schemas in mealie/schema unchanged, so the frontend
  does not need to change.
- Database access becomes async. Make routes, services and scheduler tasks async where
  they need to be.
- You may change how the tests set up and access the database (tests/conftest.py,
  tests/fixtures/, and database setup code inside test files). You must not delete tests,
  skip them, mark them xfail, remove or weaken assertions, or change expected values.
- Run the tests with: uv run pytest -n 4 --dist loadfile
  (tests in the same file depend on each other, so they must run grouped by file).
- Do not push, and do not change git remotes or git configuration.

Read AGENTS.md first, especially the "Tortoise ORM rewrite" section. Then do step C6
from that section and nothing else. Follow the conventions and decisions recorded there.
When you finish, run the tests, then update the "Tortoise ORM rewrite" section: record any
new convention or decision, and update the progress table.
```

\"date_updated\", \"last_made\"):\n        value = data.get(key)\n        if isinstance(value, str):\n            value = datetime.fromisoformat(value)\n        if isinstance(value, datetime):\n            data[key] = value.replace(tzinfo=UTC) if value.tzinfo is None else value.astimezone(UTC)\n    return data\n\n\nasync def recipe_summary(recipe_id: UUID | str | None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n    if recipe_id is None:\n        return None\n    recipe = await bridge.related_record(\"recipes\", \"id\", UUID(str(recipe_id)))\n    if recipe is None:\n        return None\n    user = await User.get_or_none(id=recipe[\"user_id\"]) if recipe[\"user_id\"] else None\n    recipe[\"household_id\"] = user.household_id if user else None\n    for field, table, association, column in (\n        (\"recipe_category\", \"categories\", \"recipes_to_categories\", \"category_id\"),\n        (\"tags\", \"tags\", \"recipes_to_tags\", \"tag_id\"),\n        (\"tools\", \"tools\", \"recipes_to_tools\", \"tool_id\"),\n    ):\n        recipe[field] = await bridge.execute(\n            f'SELECT item.* FROM \"{table}\" item JOIN \"{association}\" link '\n            f'ON item.id = link.\"{column}\" WHERE link.recipe_id = ?',\n            [UUID(str(recipe_id))],\n        )\n    for tool in recipe[\"tools\"]:\n        tool[\"households_with_tool\"] = [\n            row[\"slug\"]\n            for row in await bridge.execute(\n                \"SELECT households.slug FROM households JOIN households_to_tools link \"\n                \"ON households.id = link.household_id WHERE link.tool_id = ?\",\n                [UUID(str(tool[\"id\"]))],\n            )\n        ]\n    return _timestamps(recipe)\n\n\nasync def label(label_id: UUID | str | None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n    if label_id is None:\n        return None\n    return await bridge.related_record(\"multi_purpose_labels\", \"id\", UUID(str(label_id)))\n\n\nasync def ingredient_unit(unit_id: UUID | str | None) -> dict[str,     [food_id],\n        )\n    ]\n    food[\"substitutions\"] = await bridge.execute(\n        \"SELECT * FROM ingredient_foods_substitutions WHERE food_id = ? bridge.delete_user_dependents(user.id)\n            # The existing Alembic foreign keys have no ON DELETE CASCADE.\n            await LongLiveToken.filter(user_id=user.id).delete()\n            await PasswordResetModel.filter(user_id=user.id).delete()\n            await UserToRecipe.filter(user_id=user.id).delete()\n            await user.delete()\n            for recipe_id in recipe_ids:\n                await bridge.refresh_recipe_rating(recipe_id)\n            await self._after_write(user)\n        shutil.rmtree(PrivateUser.get_directory(result.id))\n        return result\n\n    async def get_by_username(self, username: str) -> PrivateUser | None:\n        return await self.get_one(username, \"username\")\n\n    async def get_locked_users(self) -> list[PrivateUser]:\n        return [await self._serialize(user) for user in await (await self._query()).filter(locked_at__isnull=False)]\n\n\nclass RepositoryUserRatings(AsyncUserRepository[UserRatingOut, UserToRecipe]):\n    async def _after_write(self, entry: UserToRecipe) -> None:\n        await bridge.refresh_recipe_rating(entry.recipe_id)\n        await super()._after_write(entry)\n\n    async def get_by_user(self, user_id: UUID, favorites_only: bool = False) -> list[UserRatingOut]:\n        return await self.multi_query({\"user_id\": user_id, **({\"is_favorite\": True} if favorites_only else {})})\n\n    async def get_by_recipe(self, recipe_id: UUID, favorites_only: bool = False) -> list[UserRatingOut]:\n        return await self.multi_query({\"recipe_id\": recipe_id, **({\"is_favorite\": True} if favorites_only else {})})\n\n    async def get_by_user_and_recipe(self, user_id: UUID, recipe_id: UUID) -> UserRatingOut | None:\n        results = await self.multi_query({\"user_id\": user_id, \"recipe_id\": recipe_id})\n        return results[0] if results else None\n",

}

never request input.\n    filters = {key: value, **filters}\n    conditions = \" AND \".join(f'\"{name}\" = ?' for name in filters)\n    results = await execute(f'SELECT * FROM \"{table}\" WHERE {conditions}', list(filters.values()))\n    return results[0] if results else None\n\n\nasync def query_ids(model_name: str, group_id: UUID | None, pagination: PaginationQuery) -> list[Any]:\n    model = {\n        \"User\": User,\n        \"LongLiveToken\": LongLiveToken,\n        \"PasswordResetModel\": PasswordResetModel,\n        \"UserToRecipe\": UserToRecipe,\n    }[model_name]\n    return await query_model_ids(model, group_id, pagination)\n\n\nasync def query_model_ids(\n    model: Any, group_id: UUID | None, pagination: PaginationQuery, *, household_id: UUID | None = None\n) ->        query = query.filter_by(group_id=group_id)\n    if household_id:\n        query = query.filter_by(household_id=household_id)\n    if pagination.query_filter:\n        query = ordering.split(\",\"):\n            order = order.strip()\n            try:\n                if \":\" in order:\n                    attribute, direction_value = order.split(\":\")\n ## agent_message at 2026-10-11T08:30:50.622287+00:00

C6 covers recipes plus foods, units, organizers, comments, share tokens, and timeline events. I’ve split the model conversion, recipe persistence, and async callers across agents while I handle the shared repositories and integration checks. Alembic and the final SQLAlchemy removal remain in C7.

remove_quotes_regex = re.compile(r\"\"\"['\"](.*)['\"]\"\"\")\n\n    @classmethod\n    def _normalize_search(cls, search: str, normalize_characters: bool) -> str:\n        search = search.translate(str.maketrans(cls.punctuation, \" \" * len(cls.punctuation)))\n\n        if normalize_characters:\n            search = unidecode(search).lower().strip()\n        else:\n            search = search.strip()\n\n        return search\n\n    @classmethod\n    def _build_search_list(cls, search: str) -> list[str]:\n        if cls.quoted_regex.search(search):\n            # all quoted strings\n            quoted_search_list = [match.group() for match in cls.quoted_regex.finditer(search)]\n\n            # remove outer quotes\n            quoted_search_list = [cls.remove_quotes_regex.sub(\"\\\\1\", x) for x in quoted_search_list]\n\n            # punctuation->spaces for splitting, but only on unquoted strings\n            search = cls.quoted_regex.sub(\"\", search)  # remove all quoted strings, leaving just non-quoted\n            search = search.translate(str.maketrans(cls.punctuation, \" \" * len(cls.punctuation)))\n\n            # all unquoted strings\n            unquoted_search_list = search.split()\n            search_list = quoted_search_list + unquoted_search_list\n        else:\n            search_list = search.translate(str.maketrans(cls.punctuation, \" \" * len(cls.punctuation))).split()\n\n        # remove padding whitespace inside quotes\n        return [x.strip() for x in search_list]\n\n    def   self.search_type = SearchType.tokenized\n        else:\n            self.search_type = SearchType.fuzzy\n\n        self.session = session\n        self.search = self._normalize_search(search, normalize_characters)\n        self.search_list = self._build_search_list(self.search)\n\n    def filter_query_by_search(self, query: Select, schema: type[MealieModel], model: type[SqlAlchemyBase]) ->
